I. API Overview
Casino Game API supports dierent types o games: video slots, video poker, keno, etc. and API should not be changed i
Casino Operator needs to add more games to their porolio.
As soon as new game is available, it is added to Game Library with game symbols. Casino Operator will use provided symbol
or StartGame method during opening specifc game and Bet requests will be also sent using this symbol. Game Library will
be provided to Casino Operator together with integra on package or resent when new games are available.
Using this Casino Game API Casino Operator is able to integrate both types o games: web version or desktop devices and
mobile version.
Plaorm provides the set o games built in HTML5 technology.

1.1 Seamless Wallet API
Operator should provide the Seamless Wallet Integra on API on their side. Pragma c Play will call the methods when
players make a bet or get a win and their balance should be updated.

1.5 Player ID
1.5.1 Seamless Wallet (Authenticate method)
Player ID (userId parameter) is unique iden fer o the user within Casino Operator system. Beore sending to Pragma c
Play any gaming related request Casino Operator should authen cate a player using Authen cate method. I player is new
and its account does not exist in the Pragma c Play system it will be created automa cally on the base o the data sent by
Casino Operator server in the Authen cate response. I player account already exists in the Pragma c Play database it will
be updated with the response data i necessary. Player id received in the Authen cate response will be sent with all
subsequent requests to Casino Operator.

1.5.2 Balance Transfer (StartGame method)
Player ID is unique iden fer o the user within Pragma c Play system. Beore sending to Pragma c Play any gaming related
request Casino Operator should register a player using CreatePlayer method, get player id and save it somewhere inside
own system. Then this player id will be sent with all subsequent requests to both Casino Operator and Pragma c Play.
Please note: this type o game opening remains in the API or compa bility with the previous versions o the interace.
Op on 2 is more standard and preerred way to open games or integrated systems.



1.6 Play session
Play session is a game round in which bet and wins are combined together. Each round can contain several bets, win and
reunds o the bets.
Play sessions (Rounds) that trigger ree spins or any bonus eature remain open un l the player has completed all the
ac ons required or such a eature. Un l then, the game session is incomplete and can remain open or a long me or
orever.

         Life cycle of Unfinished rounds

   Important!

   To resolve and complete unnished rounds, operators must integrate Autonaliza on feature (sec on 2.6 Auto-
   naliza on of Unnished Rounds) Autonaliza on completes rounds older than 30 days by default.


1.7 Transaction reference
Transac on reerence is unique transac on id within Pragma c Play system. Transac on reerence used or bets and wins
has to be dierent.

II. Integration API
This is a simple API or Casino Operator to retrieve some data rom Pragma c Play system and send some events to
Pragma c Play. API is an HTTP listener, which listens POST requests coming to an URL with the request mappings below.
All responses are in JSON ormat.

     URL o the HTTP service will be provided by Pragma c Play or the produc on and test environments and looks
     like:

     hps://{API service domain}/Integra onService/v3/hp/CasinoGameAPI




     Integra on simple HTTP Service is securely protected, hence please be sure:
     - player’s browser (end) must NOT be used as ini ator o requests to the service;
     - proper IPs are supplied to Pragma c Play or whitelis ng




2.1 GetCasinoGames
Request path: POST /getCasinoGames/
Using this method Casino Operator will get the list o casino games available or the integra on.

                     Possible values are:
                               GetFrbDetails
                               GetLines
                               GetDataTypes
                               GetFeatures
                               GetFcDetails

                               FilterStudio=<studio_name>, or example

    clearHistory          Specifes whether to clear the history o the round or not.

2.6 Auto-finalization of Unfinished Rounds

     Important!
     To automa cally complete unnished rounds on both the Pragma cPlay and operator side, the Autonaliza on
     func on is required for integra on.


Un nished rounds – round with in-game ree spins, or in-game bonus eatures, which was started by player but wasn’t
completed during game session.
Unfnished rounds can be caused by ollowing player’s ac ons during gameplay:

         Player hit or bought in-game ree spins and closed the game without fnishing them.
         Player hit in-game bonus game eature and closed game without fnishing it.
         Player started round in RNG table game (like American Blackjack) and closed game without comple ng the round.

Pragma c Play has the ollowing rules regarding non-fnished game rounds:
     1.         All non-fnished players’ rounds are stored in PP system.
     2.I an operator has specifc jurisdic on requirements or internal logic, the fnaliza on procedure is ac -
        vated or their brands. Meaning, all non-fnished rounds are fnalized aer a specifc period o me. The
        period o me is confgured based on jurisdic on requirements or the operator’s logic.

For Seamless Wallet API
      3.Rounds are fnalized according to the ollowing logic:
           a. I a player does not play the round at all beore closing the game, PP sends a reund request to the
              operator and closes the round (irrelevant to slots).
           b. I a player plays the round (or example, makes several ree spins) and won in any ree-spin, PP
              sends an actual win to the operator and closes the round.
           c. I a player plays the round (or example, makes several ree spins) and hasn’t won in any ree-
              spin), PP closes the round. (In case i EndRound is enabled or Operator – the Endround request
              or this round will be sent)
              In case the player makes a spin and wins addi onal ree spins or other bonus eatures (including
              bonus buy), it counts as a spin and the money is not reunded.

For Balance Transfer API
         3. Rounds are fnalized according to the ollowing logic:
             a. I a player does not complete a round beore the game closes, PP returns the bet amount to the
                player's balance and closes the round.
             b. I a player has played a round (e.g. made several reespins) and won in any reespin, PP credits a
                par al amount o the winnings to the player's balance and closes the round.
             c. I a player plays the round (or example, makes several ree spins) and hasn’t won in any ree-
                spin), player's balance is not aected. PP closes the round.
                In case the player makes a spin and wins addi onal ree spins or other bonus eatures, it counts as
                a spin and the money is not reunded.

III. Seamless Wallet API
General description
This is a simple API or Pragma c Play gaming plaorm to connect to player's wallet. API is an HTTP listener, which listens
applica on/x-www-orm-urlencoded POST requests.
In POST requests can be sent either HTTP Header:
         -   Content-Type: applica on/x-www-orm-urlencoded
         -   Content-Type: applica on/x-www-orm-urlencoded; charset=UTF-8
For applica on/x-www-orm-urlencoded, the body o the HTTP message sent to the Seamless Wallet API is essen ally one
giant query string where name/value pairs are separated by the ampersand (&), and names are separated rom values by
the equals symbol (=). An example o this would be (please see request examples or each call below):
parameter1=value1&parameter2=value2
All responses should be in JSON ormat (please see response examples or each call below).

     URL o the Seamless Wallet API should be provided by Casino Operator or the produc on and test environments.


Bonus Wallet feature
Pragma cPlay supports the 'Bonus Wallet' Feature, which allows Operators to split bet amount to “Real” and “Bonus”
parts, which will be displayed in Pragma cPlay’s Backoce summaries, respec vely. This can be managed using the 'bonus'
and 'usedPromo' parameters.

- bonus: Represents the player's available bonus balance in wallet API methods responses.
- usedPromo: Indicates the amount o bonus unds used or a specifc bet, in “Bet” API method response

For example, if a player has the following balance:
Cash: 100
Bonus: 100
When placing a 10-unit bet, the operator may respond with one of the following outcomes:

cash: 90, bonus: 100, usedPromo: 0 -> The bet was fully covered by real funds.
cash: 100, bonus: 90, usedPromo: 10 -> The bet was fully covered by bonus funds.
cash: 95, bonus: 95, usedPromo: 5 -> The bet was split, using 5 real and 5 bonus funds.

Both bonus and usedPromo parameters are required in the wallet API responses. However, i the operator does not use the
player's bonus wallet, zero values should be returned or both parameters.
Winnings or rounds in which parts o the bonus wallet were used or bets will be divided in the Pragma cPlay back oce
into real/bonus parts in the same propor ons as were used or bets in this round

                              Strictly Confden al - or intended purposes and recipients only!




3.1 Game opening
Operator can orm a valid launch URL using 3.1.2 GameURL API method

   Operator can allow or decline player to par cipate in promo campaigns (incl. FSB, Tournament and Prize Drop
   campaigns). Two way are possible:
          1. By sending exraInfo/ promoAvailable parameter in authen cate response (It has higher priority)
          2. By sending promo parameter in launch URL (It has lower priority)
   It is strongly recommended to use      exraInfo/ promoAvailable parameter in authen cate response!



   Logic opromo parameter applica on or promo campaigns (incl. FSB, Tournament and Prize Drop campaigns)
       
            Public promo campaigns: parameter value is applied or all players;
       
            Private promo campaigns with        include list: Player which is in include list will be able to par cipate
            regardless o parameter value. Rest o players are unable to par cipate;
       
            Private promo campaigns with      exclude list: Player which is in exclude list will not be able to par cipate
            regardless o parameter value. Rest o players par cipate depending on parameter value

3.2 Hash calculation
Hash code is calculated with ollowing ormula:
From request post parameters, all parameters are taken (except hash) and appending to string:
      1. Sort all parameter by keys in alphabe cal order.
      2. Append them in key1=value1&key2=value2.
      3. Append secret key, e.g.: key1=value1&key2=value2SECRET.
      4. Calculate the hash by using MD5.
      5. Compare with hash parameter. In the case o ailure Casino Operator should send the error code 5.

3.4 Authenticate
Request path: POST /authenticate.html
When the game is opening Pragma c Play receives with URL security token generated by Casino Operator. Using this token
Pragma c Play will ask Casino Operator or player authen ca on and get the player’s balance.


Impor an :Token rom Authen cate response can be used in subsequent wallet requests or other games, launched
rom the inside o game or bingo client.



Pragma c Play can send subsequent authen cate requests with the same token, when player:
- opens new casino game rom built-in mini lobby
- opens bingo room rom bingo lobby
- opens live casino game rom live casino lobby
Sending addi onal Authen cate requests or inner opening is op onal         and is not handled by the Pragma c Play by
default. In case Casino Operator need to receive addi onal authoriza on for inner opening - they should ask the
Pragma c Play’s Technical Support for congura on.
Operator should accept addi onal authen cate requests with ac ve token or player.

3.10 EndRound

game round transac ons on their side in real me.

     unc onality on the Pragma c Play side. Instead, Data Feed API can be used or transac on fnaliza on, in order

IV. Balance Transfer API
This is a simple API or game Operator to connect to the Pragma c Play game system. API is an HTTP listener, which listens
POST requests coming to an URL with the request mappings below.
All responses should be in JSON ormat and should contain error code and error descrip on, which is empty in the case i no
error occurs.

     URL o the HTTP service will be provided by Pragma c Play or the produc on and test environments and looks
     like:

     hps://{API service domain}/Integra onService/v3/hp/CasinoGameAPI




     Balance Transer HTTP service is securely protected, hence please be sure:
     - player’s browser (end) must NOT be used as ini ator o requests to the service;
     - proper IPs are supplied to Pragma c Play or whitelis ng




      It is possible to play only one porolio at the same me with this API.


4.1 Game opening
Aer Operator calls StartGame method at Pragma c Play side he gets an URL or game opening.
The link will look like:
hps://{game server domain}/gs2c/openGame.do?tc={ cket}&stylename={secureLogin}
   where:
       game server domain – a domain name or game opening and playing;
         cket – secure one- me token generated by Pragma c Play.



4.2 Hash calculation
Hash code is calculated with ollowing ormula:
From request post parameters, all parameters are taken (except hash) and appending to string:
      1. Sort all parameter by keys in alphabe cal order.
      2. Append them in key1=value1&key2=value2.
      3. Append secret key, e.g.: key1=value1&key2=value2SECRET.
      4. Calculate the hash by using MD5.
      5. Compare with hash parameter. In the case o ailure Casino Operator should send the error code 5.

                                                   YOUR FAVOURITE EVERY TIME
                         Strictly Confden al - or intended purposes and recipients only!

V. Games JavaScript API
This sec on describes Pragma c Games JS API which can be used by operators to take dierent events like spin started,
ended, entering to bonus game or ree spins directly rom the game using JavaScript communica on. Star ng the game
launch, Pragma c games send dierent events to the topmost window, so they can be          caught and used or dierent
purposes on operator side – to aract players’ aen on, etc.



5.1 Game Events Interception
During lie me, star ng the moment when game is loaded into irame, game sends dierent events based on user behavior
to the topmost window. So that, sent events can be intercepted on operator side and used urther.
The example o the code which can intercept these events looks like this:
XD.receiveMessage(handler, ["hp://", $Host].join(""));
XD.receiveMessage(handler, ["hps://", $Host].join(""));
Where handler - js custom unc on or handling the event
           XD - the postMessage library
All events are sent in the ollowing ormat:
Object {
name:<name o the event>
data:<addi onal data or this event>
};


Example:
Object {
name: "bonusGameStarted"
};

VI. Variable Free Spins API
    Free Rounds Bonus (FRB) has been renamed to         Free Spins Bonus (FSB) .
    Note that the change only aects the method name and has no eect on the parameters, content, or API methods
    as a whole.


Free Spins Bonus (FSB) (previously Free Rounds Bonus (FRB)) allows the player to play a specifed number o ree spins in a
game. Free Spins created via Variable Free Spins API may be confgured to be played with a certain bet value specifed or
each game and currency. During Free Spins game play, bets are not deducted rom the player's balance, but all wins are
collected or the Free Spins Bonus in the Pragma c Play’s system.
Aer all ree spins are played the Pragma c Play system sends a bonusWin API call to Operator server to add money to the
player's balance. Please note that not all games support FSB.
Free Spins can be awarded to players registered within the Pragma c Play system using API.
Free Spins Bonus HTTP service is a simple API or game Operator to manage Free Spins. API is an HTTP listener, which listens
POST requests coming to an URL with the request mappings below.
I the Free Spins API is used to create the FSB, then implemen ng cancel via the API (method 6.4 Cancel Free Spins) is also
mandatory.
All responses should be in JSON ormat.

     URL o the FSB HTTP service will be provided by Pragma c Play or the produc on and test environments and
     looks like: hps://{API service domain}/Integra onService/v3/hp/FreeRoundsBonusAPI/v2/




     Free Spins HTTP service is securely protected, hence please be sure:
     - player’s browser (end) must NOT be used as ini ator o requests to the service;
     - proper IPs are supplied to Pragma c Play or whitelis ng



    The bonusCode is unique and can be used only one me or same playerId. Please make sure you’ve integrated the
    FSB in the proper way and won't reuse/award same   bonusCode values in your logic. In case Casino Operator needs
    to make the bonusCode reusable they should ask the Pragma c Play’s Technical Support or addi onal
    confgura on.



6.1 Hash calculation
Hash code is calculated with ollowing ormula:
From request post parameters, all parameters are taken (except hash) and appending to string:
    1. Sort all parameter by keys in alphabe cal order.
    2. Append them (i the value is not null or empty) in key1=value1&key2=value2.
    3. Append secret key, e.g.: key1=value1&key2=value2SECRET.
    4. Calculate the hash by using MD5.
    5. Compare with hash parameter. In the case o ailure Casino Operator should send the error code 5.

                                                   YOUR FAVOURITE EVERY TIME
                               Strictly Confden al - or intended purposes and recipients only!

6.4 Cancel Free Spins
Request path: POST /FreeRoundsBonusAPI/v2/bonus/cancel/
Using this method Operator can cancel an exis ng Free Spins Bonus in the Pragma c Play system. In addi on to campaign
cancella on, all related players' bonuses will also be cancelled according to the corresponding logic.

      Cancella on logic or bonuses depends on the bonus status.
      All not-started bonuses will be cancelled.

      I the bonus was started but not fnished:
                or ree spins and countdown s, already played spins and awarded wins remain, but the rest o the spins and ree
                seconds will be cancelled,
                single unfnished rounds, such as instant bonuses or ree spin rounds, will not be cancelled, so the player can
                fnish them and receive wins later.

      Finished, cancelled, or expired bonuses will not be aected (the player’s win and bonus status remain).

    secureLogin                User name or authen ca on in the Casino Game API service                                  Required
    bonusCode                  Bonus id within the Operator system. Should be unique within the brand.                    Required
    hash                        Hash code o the request.                                                                 Required



Response parameters

    error                      Error code.
    descrip on                 Descrip on o the error or troubleshoo ng.

6.8 Remove players
Request path: POST /FreeRoundsBonusAPI/v2/players/remove/
Using this method Operator can remove players rom the exis ng Free Spins Bonus and cancel ree spins awarded to them.

      Cancella on logic or bonuses depends on the bonus status.
      All not-started bonuses will be cancelled.

      I the bonus was started but not fnished:
                or ree spins and countdown s, already played spins and awarded wins remain, but the rest o the spins and ree
                seconds will be cancelled,
                single unfnished rounds, such as instant bonuses or ree spin rounds, will not be cancelled, so the player can
                fnish them and receive wins later.

      Finished, cancelled, or expired bonuses will not be aected (the player’s win and bonus status remain).

                                separated.
    requestID                  Unique iden fer o FSB credit request (or specifc player) which was sent                         Op onal

    hash                        Hash code o the request.                                                                        Required

6.11 Error codes
The table below contains the error codes that the Pragma c Play system will return in the response to Variable Free Spins
API calls.
   Code       Descripton

     0        Request was successully processed.

     1        Authen ca on ailed. Incorrect secure login and secure password combina on.

     2        Valida on ailed. Empty mandatory feld '{feld name}'.

     3        Game(s) are not supported: {list o the game id}.

     4        Game(s) do not support Free round bonus: {list o the game id}

     5        Bonus code already exists.

     6        The requested Free Round bonus is not ound in the system.

     7        Free round bonus is canceled.

     8        Free round bonus is closed or started to play.

     9        Currency code '{ISO code}' is incorrect or unsupported.

     10       Player does not have ac ve Free round bonuses.

     11       Free round bonus cannot be created. Expira on date limit (30 days) has been exceeded.

     12       Free round bonus cannot be created. Expira on date is in the past.

     14       Bonus code already exists with another parameters.

     19       Free round bonus is expired.

     37       Bad request: periodOTime and rounds cannot be used in same          me

   37#1       Bad request: Either dates (expira onDate and validityDate) or periods (expira onPeriod and
              validityPeriod) should be specifed.

   37#2       Bad request: I expira onPeriod is specifed validityPeriod must be specifed as well.

   37#3       Bad request: Expira on period limit ({X} days) has been exceeded.

   37#4       Bad request: The dierence between Validity period and Expira on period should not exceed the limit
              ({Y} days).

   37#5       Bad request: Expira on period exceeds the Validity period.

   37#6       Bad request: playerList' size should be less or equals to 5000

     38       Bet per line less than 0.01 aer total bet conversion (totalBet / number o slot lines)

     39       Bet per line less than 0.01

     44       Package is not ound or cancelled.

     45       Package is expired.

     46       Player already registered in package with same bonusCode.

   1000       Internal server error. Pragma c Play will return this error code i their system has internal problem and
              cannot process the request.

VII. Game History API
History API provides game rounds played by the player, with their details. Operator can get a list o games played, game
rounds that the player has played during a par cular day and hour, and the screen o the game at the end o the game
round.
History HTTP service is a simple API or game Operator to get player’s game history. API is an HTTP listener, which listens
POST requests coming to an URL with the request mappings below.
All responses should be in JSON ormat.

     URL o the history HTTP service will be provided by Pragma c Play or the produc on and test environments and
     looks like:

     hps://{API service domain}/Integra onService/v3/hp/HistoryAPI/




     History HTTP service is securely protected, hence please be sure:
     - player’s browser (end) must NOT be used as ini ator o requests to the service;
     - proper IPs are supplied to Pragma c Play or whitelis ng



   IMPORTANT!
   For RNG games porolio, all Game History API methods are supported
   For LC, VSB porolio only 7.7 OpenHistoryExtended and 7.6 GetRoundStatus methods are supported



7.1 Hash calculation
Hash code is calculated with ollowing ormula:
From request post parameters, all parameters are taken (except hash) and appending to string:
      1. Sort all parameter by keys in alphabe cal order.
      2. Append them (i the value is not null or empty) in key1=value1&key2=value2.
      3. Append secret key, e.g.: key1=value1&key2=value2SECRET.
      4. Calculate the hash by using MD5.
      5. Compare with hash parameter. In the case o ailure Casino Operator should send the error code 5.




                                                     © 2025 Pragmatc Play
                                                   YOUR FAVOURITE EVERY TIME
                             Strictly Confden al - or intended purposes and recipients only!

VIII. Data feeds and reports
Data eeds let Casino Operator to download inorma on about in-game money transac ons or all game rounds played and
jackpot winnings.
In order to receive DataFeeds data or casino, Operator should ollow the ow below:
     1. Call /environments API method with relevant secureLogin iden fer or casino
     2. In response Pragma cPlay will return a list o environment and direct API domains (single or mul ple environment
          domains can be returned)
     3. Operator should call DataFeeds API method rom each Environment, using the relevant API domain
     4. In case o mul ple domains are returned in Environment API response, Operator should aggregate DataFeeds
          received rom dierent environments, on their side

Data o the eeds related to large number o records (like transac ons) will be requested using         me point and sent to the
Operator in CSV ormat.

     For each API account, frequency of any par cular "Data feeds and reports" method requests, should be not more
     than one call per minute.

     Also, the data can be extracted no later than the last month.




     Any "Data feeds and reports" method should be requested                   only with dataType parameter value, which is
     available for Operator      . Available dataType values can be received from 2.1     2.1 GetCasinoGames     method



Time points
Time point is a mestamp star ng rom the server me when data is requested or the frst                me. I the request is sent
without a me point, the system returns a me point, which should be sent with the next request.
Whenever a request with the me point is sent, data rom the moment o mepoint to the current moment will be
transerred, along with the new me point. This new me point should be used or the next request, and so on.
Using such a mechanism, no data will be lost. I clients haven’t received response rom the Pragma c Play with the new        me
point, they will be able to re-send the request with the same me point, un l requested data is received.



     Data can be loaded by small por ons or periods o         me not longer than 10 minutes.




Using timepoints for past periods
I Operator needs to get data or a longer period they can send a number o subsequent requests seng up a star ng
  mepoint and then using mepoints that the Pragma c Play system returns in the response.
For example, i Operator needs to get data or the last N minutes, they can calculate the start date and me o the period as
Start = (“now” – N minutes ), convert it into mestamp and send request to the Transac ons API using this mestamp as a
  me point. Server will return data or 10 minutes star ng rom the mestamp sent in the request, along with the new me
point. Using mepoints returned by the server and sending requests one aer another Operator can get as many por ons
o data as they need, to download data that are already in the past.

Data formats

All successul server responses in CSV ormat will contain a new me point that should be sent then by the client to the
server in the next request. Format o the server response is as ollows:
#timepoint = {server time in ms}
{Data in standard csv format}

Client should get the frst line rom the response, parse it and save the   me point somewhere or the next request. The rest
o the response should be parsed as normal CSV stream.

Hash calculation
Hash code is calculated with ollowing ormula:
From request GET parameters, all parameters are taken (except hash) and appending to string:
      1. Sort all parameter by keys in alphabe cal order.
      2. Append them (i the value is not null or empty) in key1=value1&key2=value2.
      3. Append secret key, e.g.: key1=value1&key2=value2SECRET.
      4. Calculate the hash by using MD5.

Flow details
The game ow is shown in the example o the slot.
    1.  Player clicks Spin buon on the game screen, and game client sends doSpin request to the Pragma c Play Game
        server.
    2. Game server calls Pragma c Play Integra on service to withdraw money rom the player balance.
    3. Integra on service sends Bet request to the Operator server. I the request cannot be handled by Operator due to
        connec on problem, Pragma c Play server will repeat the request 3 mes. Aer that Reund request will be sent
        to the Operator server or cancelling bet un l response rom Operator server is received.
    4. Operator verifes that player has enough unds in the wallet to make a bet, subtracts requested amount rom the

        returns an error.

        bonus game eature, calculates win amount, etc.

        lines, triggered eatures and win amount.

        server.

    13. Game server collects player’s win.
    14. Game server returns response to the game client containing updated player balance.

    16. Game server starts bonus game eature.
    17. Game server returns response with all inorma on that is necessary or the game client to play bonus game.
    18. Game client sends a request to the game server on every step o the bonus game.
    19. Game server handles request and returns response with the result o current step.
    20. When bonus game is over game client sends doCollectBonus request to the Game server.
    21. Game server calls Pragma c Play Integra on service to add bonus game win amount to the player balance.
    22. Integra on service calls Operator server, sending BetResult request. I request cannot be handled by Operator due
        to connec on problem, Pragma c Play server will repeat request un l it receives a response rom Operator server.
    23. Operator adds money to player's wallet and returns updated player balance.
    24. Pragma c Play Integra on service returns updated balance to the Game server.

XII. Reconciliation
12.1 Reconciliation process
I a request mes out because o internet connec on problems, or contains relevant error code (             3.17 Error codes ), then the
Pragma c Play system will ollow a process described below, to reconcile the ac on.

    For the cases where Operator receives retry API calls within reconcilia on process, actual player's balance must be
    returned in response


Bet transaction
The Bet request will be retried 2 (two)   mes with a delay o 5 seconds between aempts.
I a request is s ll not processed aer the second try, Pragma c Play will ini ate the Reund request to cancel this single bet
on the Operator side.

Refund transaction
Reund request will be added to a Transac on queue and an error message will be displayed to the player on the game
interace. The game client should be closed. The reconcilia on mechanism starts rom there.
Operator will accept Reund requests that run independently o the game session in order to ensure the two systems are
kept in sync.
Such a request can be sent i Pragma c Play has an unknown response on the Reund request on its side because o a
  meout or relevant error code (so it will try to run it in the background un l the Operator response will not reach
Pragma c Play).
Operator will return a successul response on the Reund request even i the transac on has been canceled in the past and
does not need to be canceled again or even i Bet has not reached Operator system because o meouts.

Winning transactions
The BetResult/BonusWin/JackpotWin/Adjustment requests will be retried 2 (two)       mes with a delay o 5 seconds between
aempts.
I a request is s ll not processed aer the second try then it will be added to the Transac on queue or reconcilia on. Game
round result will be displayed to the player on the game interace.
BetResult/BonusWin/JackpotWin/Adjustment requests will be added to the Transac on queue and sent independently o
the game session, according to the Reconcilia on mechanism.

PromoWin
The PromoWin requests will be retried in the similar way as described calls above but with a delay o 30 minutes aer the
tournament is going to be fnished.

EndRound
The EndRound request will be added to the Transac on queue once the game server determines a game round is over. The
reconcilia on mechanism starts rom there.
Operator will accept EndRound requests that run independently o the game session in order to ensure the two systems are
kept in sync. Repeated EndRound request can be sent only i the Pragma c Play system has an unknown status on its side
because o a meout or special error code on the EndRound call, so it will try to run it in the background un l the Operator
response will not reach Pragma c Play.




                                                      © 2025 Pragmatc Play
                                                    YOUR FAVOURITE EVERY TIME
                               Strictly Confden al - or intended purposes and recipients only!




12.2 Reconciliation mechanism
The reconcilia on mechanism ensures the two systems (Game Plaorm and Casino Operator) are kept in sync.
From the Pragma c Play system perspec ve, the Transac on queue is a table within the database that records any Reund,
BetResult/BonusWin/JackpotWin/PromoWin/Adjustment and EndRound requests that med out.
The Pragma c Play system put in place an automated process (cron job) that reads the Transac on queue table rom the
database and runs the requests within the queue.
Aer 24 hours, the requests that are s ll unsuccessul will be marked or manual reconcilia on.

                    the predefned value: rcHistoryUrl=ingame
    o    rcCloseUrl – a link to the page on the Operator’s website, to which the player will be redirected i they choose to
         close the game. This parameter is op onal. The “Close” buon appears on the reality check message only i
         Operator sends the URL
The “Con nue” buon always appears on the reality check message. This buon removes the reality check message and

XIV. Custom messages
Seamless Wallet API responses can op onally contain one or more messages that will be shown in the player’s game client.
This op onal eature ini ally is designed or UKGC CMA support, but operators can also use it or their needs.
Upon receiving a message, game client will display inorma on to the player in order to inorm him or her about a specifc
event, and op onally request a user response.
All text that is sent inside a message must be translated to the player’s language.



14.1 Custom messages syntax
A custom message is an op onal “message” element added to API responses. There can be zero or more “message”
elements per response:
{…
  "messageTriggers": [
  {
    "title": "Starting to use bonus funds",
     "text": "You are starting play with bonus funds.\nFrom this point it possible that won funds
may have to be\nre-bet many times before they can be withdrawn from your account.\nPlease click
Bonus Details for further details.",
    "nonIntrusive": false,
    "options": [
    {
      "action": "continue",
      "label": "OK"
    },
    {
      "action": "link",
      "label": "Bonus Details"
      "linkType": "redirect",
      "url": " https://www.casino.com/bonus/details",
     } ]
  } ]
}


Each item in the “ messageTriggers         ” array may contain elements      title, text, nonIntrusive and options .

The title element contains the tle o the message box, which must be in one short line o no more than 60 characters.
The text specifes the message box text and may contain mul ple lines. When speciying a orced line break the symbol
used is \n. The max length o the text is 300 characters.
InonIntrusive is specifed and true, the message can be shown in a non-modal, non-intrusive ashion. Deault value: alse.
The options array can contain one or more elements. Each op on always has   action and label elements and may contain
op onal url element or redirect or Ajax call and a linkType speciying how the request should be sent to the URL i it is
specifed. Op ons and link types are specifed below.

                                               “ac on” parameter.

                                               game ow.

15.3 Launch games from internal lobby for Italian regulated market
Since Italian regulator requires operators to ask players or unds that they want to spend playing a certain game, a game
provider, who oer an in-game mini lobby, have to implement the same in their games.
There are two things that are important with this regard, once player has chosen a game:
    1.   A total balance should be shown to the player in a pop up, in which he will enter the amount he wants to spend in
         this game
    2.   The current game session must be closed and a new game session opened on operator’s side and communicated
         to AAMS.
Also, in order to display current AAMS Ticket and AAMS Sessionid in game client, operator should send ollowing addi onal
parameters in authen cate response (in extraIno response parameter body):

                                  “aamsTicket”: “someaams cketvalue”;
                                  “aamsSessionId: “someaamssessionidvalue”


Pragma cPlay oer mini lobby or Live games and slots. Both types o games can be enhanced to support Italian
requirements. To make it possible, the ollowing changes to PP Na ve Wallet API are proposed:
    1.   New “totalBalance” parameter will be added to the response on both /balance and /getBalancePerGame calls
         (depending on which one is used by operator in exis ng integra on). The parameter is op onal, should be sent by
         operator or Italy but can be sent or all players as well.
    2.   The ollowing parameters will be sent to Authen cate request, which will inorm operator that a new game is
         about to be opened rom in-game lobby:
             o launchingType - indicates the way the game is being launched. Possible values are: “N” - normally (by
                   operator), “L” - rom in-game lobby. The deault value is “N”
             o gameId- id o the game being launched
             o chosenBalance - amount o money player wants to spend in the game
             o previousToken - value i the token that was received rom operator or the game that should be closed
                   (corresponds to the current opened game session on operator’s side)
    3.   Parameter “token” will be empty i a new game is being launched rom in-game mini lobby. Token or this game
         should be generated by operator and returned in the response
When operator gets an Authen ca on call with launchingType=L, they should do the ollowing:
    1.   Close the current game session iden fed by previousToken parameter in operator’s system
    2.   Close AAMS cket or that game session
    3.   Open a new game session in operator’s system
    4.   Open a new cket in AAMS
    5.   Return new token, player id, his balance, etc. in the response as usually

15.4 Portuguese Regulated Market - Slot Machine and RNG Table Game Reporting
This is a simple API or Operators to connect to the Pragma c Play gaming system. API is an HTTP listener, which listens
POST requests coming to an URL with the request mappings below.

     URL o the HTTP service will be provided by Pragma c Play or the produc on and test environments and looks
     like: hps://{API service domain}/Regula onService/



     The service is securely protected, hence please be sure:
     - player’s browser (end) must NOT be used as ini ator o requests to the service;
     - proper IPs are supplied to Pragma c Play or whitelis ng




Introduction
This document describes the API, which Pragma c Play has created or Operators in Portugal, or slot machines and RNG
table games repor ng according to SRIJ requirements providing the way o geng the string needed to fll the smResult
feld present in the AJOG fles.
         API Archi ec ure
The communica on interace between the Operator and the Pragma c Play is perormed by server-to-server requests via
HTTPS (using POST method). Responses are sent as JSON. The structure and the elements inorma on are defned in this
sec on.
         Reques s Au hentcaton
All requests that are sent to the Pragma c Play API should be authen cated. Each request contains Secure Login as a
request parameter and a Password as a part o the hash code calculated based on the request parameters. Pragma c Play
will supply the Operator with dedicated creden als.
         Error Handling
I the ErrorCode parameter equals to 0 or not exist, then the response was processed successully with no error. In Case the
ErrorCode > 0, it means that the response was processed with an error.

Slot Machine and RNG Table Game Reporting API
         Hash calculaton
Hash code is calculated with the ollowing ormula:
From request post parameters, all parameters are taken (except hash) and appending to string:
    1.   Sort all parameter by keys in alphabe cal order.
    2.   Append them (i the value is not null or empty) in key1=value1&key2=value2.
    3.   Append secret key, e.g.: key1=value1&key2=value2SECRET.
    4.   Calculate the hash by using MD5.
    5.   Compare with hash parameter. In the case o ailure Casino Operator should send the error code 1.
Creden als or connec ng to Pragma c Play service, or integra on and produc on environments, will be provided by our
Integra on team by Operator’s request.

15.6 Promo campaigns requirements for regulated markets
According to the new requirements (e.g Spanish cer fca on requirements or Dutch Gaming Authority / KSA), an operator
needs to authen cate every player beore leng them access any promo campaign (incl. FSB, Tournament and Prize Drop
campaigns). Players need to meet promo on criteria set by the par cular regulator beore par cipa ng in a promo
campaign. That is why operator should send player’s status (is player eligible or promo or not) to Pragma c Play beore
game suggests any promo ons to that player. I player is eligible, player will receive a no fca on about exis ng
promo ons and can take part in it. I player is not eligible, no promo no fca ons will be displayed.
Solutons:
Operator can no y Pragma cPlay system about player’s promo-access status in both ways, by special parameter in game
launch URL or special parameter in authen cate response


     It is recommended For Casino Operator to use       ex raInfo parame er in au hentca e response op on, as this way is
     secure and excludes possibility o promo access status control rom player’s side.




     ex raInfoparameter in authen cate response has priority over       promo parameter in game launch URL



1.   ex raInfo parame er in au hentca e response

When player opens any o the Pragma c Play games, Operator needs to send the specifc parameterin authen cate
response which indicates player’s promo status "extraInfo":{"promoAvailable":"y"}

         I promoAvailable: ”y” – it means that player is eligible or promo ons and promo can be suggested to that player.
         I promoAvailable: “n” – it means that player is not eligible or promo ons and promo should not be suggested to
         that player.

2.   promo parame er in game launch URL
Every me player opens any o the Pragma c Play games, Operator needs to send the specifc parameter with player’s
promo status. The new parameter promo will be added into launcher with possible values y – yes/n – no.

         I promo = y – it means that player is eligible or promo ons and promo can be suggested to that player.
         I promo = n – it means that player is not eligible or promo ons and promo should not be suggested to that
         player.

Hash calculation

Hash code is calculated with ollowing ormula:
From request post parameters, all parameters are taken (except hash) and appending to string:
      6. Sort all parameter by keys in alphabe cal order.
      7. Append them in key1=value1&key2=value2.
      8. Append secret key, e.g.: key1=value1&key2=value2SECRET.
      9. Calculate the hash by using MD5.
      10. Compare wi h hash parame er. In he case of failure Casino Opera or should send he error code 5.




Desk op version o bingo client should have     minimal width or wrapper (irame) 840dp and height 500dp, so that content
can be ully displayed. In mobile version, or phones and tablets, the bingo client support min window width 320dp.
Our Backend allows to disable some elements in bingo client depending on its version:
    1.   For Mobile version:
    -    Mul Tabs – no switching between rooms within mobile bingo client interace;
    -    Balance – no balance with deposit buon within mobile bingo client interace;
    -    Menu – no menu buon within mobile bingo client interace;
    -    Mini-games lobby – no mini games unc onality within mobile bingo client interace;
    -    Chat – no chang unc onality;
    2.   For Desk op version:
    -    Header – no header within desktop bingo client interace replacing balance and menu to interace below, no bingo
         lobby unc onality;
    -    Menu - no menu buon within desktop bingo client interace;
    -    Balance – no balance feld within desktop bingo client interace;
    -    Mini-games lobby – no mini games unc onality within desktop bingo client interace;
    -    Chat - no chat area within desktop bingo client interace;


In order to access dierent sengs and launch bingo client inside irame on Operator websites, the ollowing cases with
custom messages are available:
    -    { name: 'no yCloseContainer' } - is sent or any closing event inside bingo client which no fes main window that
         container with bingo client should be closed.

         this Operator`s site should response with post message { type: 'rooms', roomIds: [...] }, with rooms which are avail-

Mini-games
Bingo client allows OPERATOR to introduce user to Bingo, video slots and other games by using irame in special area inside
web applica on.
In order to do that, the OPERATOR needs to create a special lobby page or mini-games list (should be hosted on OPERATOR
side), which will allow player to choose and play any mini-game inside Bingo client.
Irame`s size or lobby page in the deskop version should have non-fxed height but alterable width between 720dp and
480dp. Mini-game will be launched in special container as separate irame, by post message no fca on rom lobby-page to
main window, in moment when user clicks to launch a min-game. The aspect ra o between width and height is 16:9 or
mini-game container in each resolu on.
Irame`s size or lobby page in the mobile version has alterable width between 320dp and higher, depends on device
display size, an aspect ra o o irame between width and height is 16:9.
OPERATOR needs to implement special post messages logic or Bingo web client page.
Implemenaton of pos messages by he dispached even explanaton:

‘otherWindow’ can listen to dispatched messages by execu ng the ollowing JavaScript:

window.addEventListener("message", receiveMessage, false);

function receiveMessage(event)
{
  if (event.origin !== "http://example.org:8080")
    return;

    // ...
}



    NOTE: External link rom the Operator side should always begin with HTTPS. CORS headers should always be present.

Example o implementa on on OPERATOR side:
<div class="mini-games__item" >
  <div
    class="mini-games__item-img"
     style="background-image: url(/resources/mini-slots/madame-des ny.png)"
     data-link="hps://{game server domain}/gs2c/playGame.do?key=<KEY_VALUE>&stylename={secureLogin}&gameSym-
bol=vs10madame&minimode=1"
  ></div>
</div>

Bingo client expects receive next:
window.postMessage(JSON.stringify({ type: 'OPEN_SLOT', link:          <link of launcher> }), '*'); – when game is launching on
OPERATOR lobby page should be sent such post message.
For game Providers o mini-games to sync upda ng o balance between bingo and game, we recommend to support next
post message: postMessage( { name: 'updateBalance' }, '*' ). This post message will be sent by bingo client to child window
with mini-game container to update balance inorma on.


For more inorma on use the ollowing documenta on:
hps://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/Window/postMessage

Free Spins Bonus Prizes

This eature allows operator to set prize type as Free Spins Bonus (FSB), which urther can be used by player in
Pragma cPlay slot games.
In order to support Spinner room with FSB prizes in BINGO product, In    3.8 Resul, 3.11 EndRound , 3.8 BonusWin methods
will be added new op onal array specPrizes[#] with next parameters inside:

          specPrizeAmount – number o FSB which was won by player in bingo game
          specPrizeCode – uniqual FSB bonus code within PP system per bingo game and player.
          specPrizeType – “FSB” which will be special mark that this prize is Free Spins bonus or casino game
This list is op onal and is not sent to the Casino Operator by deault. In case Casino Operator needs this parameter to be
sent with request they should ask the Pragma c Play’s Technical Support or addi onal confgura on. Such result should be
sent only i player won FSB with other monetary prize. Otherwise i players has only FSB prize in current game, this list
should be taken rom endRound and result is not sent. I Operator`s players par cipate in bingo games with FSB prizes but
this list is not set up, result won`t be sent at all and round will be closed without winners on Operator side.

17.3 Bingo integration API for PROVIDER side
Hash calculation
In Provider side API security hash is passed as special request header x-hash
Consider the RoomList request as an example o hash calcula on.

     NOTE: parameter names should be taken or calcula on by order rom json request



Phase 1: Get request body
Example:
{
     "language": "en",
     "playerID": "1677d81655c46",
     "secureLogin": "secure_login"
}



Phase 2: Add ‘secure password’ to request body string
Example:
{
    "language": "en",
    "playerID": "1677d81655c46",
    "secureLogin": "secure_login"
}secure_password



Phase 3 Encode MD5
Example:
62e80083e2016890d33aae54a240d


Phase 4: Add x-hash header to the request
Example:
POST /BingoIntegration/BingoGameAPI/RoomList HTTP/1.1
Host: api-domain
-H 'x-hash: 62e80083e2ffb016890d33aae54a240d'
-H ‘Content-Type: application/json’
  -d '{
    "language": "en",
    "playerID": "1677d81655c46",
    "secureLogin": "secure_login"
}'

Error Codes
Tables below contain the error codes that should be returned by PROVIDER in the response.
Shared excep ons:

    Code         Descripton

         0       "OK"

       100       Internal Service Error

         5       Incorrect secureLogin or/and securePassword

         7       Required feld is empty: '{0}' is required

         8       Field {0} is wrong. {0} will contain a feld causing an error.

         9       Player with transerred nickname already exists

        10       Player is not ound

        11       Currency code '{0}' is incorrect or unsupported



Bingo excep ons:

    Code         Descripton

       101       You cannot order cards more than max available number

       102       Pre-order o cards is not allowed at this point o   me or the selected game

       103       This game is not available anymore. Please order cards to another game

       105       The requested data is not ound

       106       The cards were not ound according to the criteria

       107       incorrect number o cards

         1       Insucient balance

       109       Wallet transac on ailed

       110       Max number o cards are already bought or this round


Bonus service excep ons:
    Code         Descripton

       202       Request problem. Empty mandatory feld '%s'. '%s' will contain a feld causing an error

       203       Room(s) are not supported: %s. '%s' will contain ID o a room / rooms causing an error

       204       Game(s) do not support FR bonus: %s. '%s' will contain ID o a game / games causing an error

18.1 Live Casino Dynamic Games Access
DGA Overview
DGA stands or Dynamic Games Access.
DGA is a data transer system that will provide our operators with game table data to populate thumbnails on their website.
Func onality will use HTML Websockets protocol to transer the data to the operator’s website in real me. Data will be
consumed in JSON ormat.

WebSocket data feed
Pragma cplay will provide the operator with a JavaScript API (.js fle) to handle the HTML Websocket protocol. Using this
API an operator will be able to make a successul subscribe to the websockets and also have unc ons available to listen
and consume the incoming data. Doing this will allow an operator a very easy way o managing the data on their website.

Data feeds
    The type o inorma on that will be delivered to an operator through the DGA system will be the ollowing:
         Table Name, language specifc table ino & image.
         Table Limits
         Table Open/Closed
         NewTable(true/alse) (i new table is createdand in less than 30 days, will be passed newTable:true , otherwise -
         newTable:alse)
         Dealer Name
         Roulee
             o Last 20 results
             o Updated aer each game round
             o Number o players in the game
         Baccarat
             o Shue no fca ons
             o Last 21 results
             o Updated aer every game
             o baccaratShoeSummary - Provides the total number o completed games, along with a breakdown o
                  results: number o Player wins, Banker wins, Tie outcomes, as well as the count o Player Pair and Banker
                  Pair occurrences
         Blackjack
             o Seats taken/seats open
         One Blackjack
             o Number o players in the game
         Game Shows
             o Last 20 results
                            Slots o mul pliers (i applicable)
             o Number o players in the game
         Mega Sic Bac

JavascriptAPI
Using PP’s javascript API the operator will be able to handle the DGA data much easier. Depending on the chosen push or
pull unc onality, the .js fle will include unc ons to subscribe, listen and consume data. The API will include the source
code fle(.js) and also how to use the unc ons provided
Latest js fle can be ound on:
                     hps://dga.pragma cplaylive.net/dgaAPI.js

Example of he functons con ained inside he API

To start consuming the game inorma on rom Pragma c Play’s tables you will need to ollow a ew steps:
          Step1
                   dga.connect(‘server’)
                             Server could be set to live or staging URL. Pragma c Play will provide this.
                             1 server connec on .
                   dga.available(‘casinoID’)
                             Request will return all tables available or selected casino. Pragma c Play will provide the
                             relevant casinoID once integra on o DGA starts.
                   dga.subscribe(‘casinoID', ‘tableID‘, ’currency’);
                             Mul ple subscribe methods. 1 subscribe method or each table you would like to get ino rom.
                             Connect to a table to receive table inorma on. tableIDs will be provided.
                             Currency is op onal. When empty EUR values will be sent. Please consult Pragma c Play or
                             supported currencies.
                   dga.onConnect()
                             When dga.connect(‘server’) has successully connected to DGA server, then the dga.onConnect()
                             method can be invoked. This can then be used to contain all the subscribe methods.
          Step 2
                   dga.onMessage(data)
                             To consume data. Data will be contained as an object or ease o use. Override this method to
                             start using the response inorma on.
                             Please see Data ormat & object example on the next page. Every me the table ino updates a
                             new object will be send with the updated inorma on to be consumed
          Step 3
                   dga.disconnect()
                             disconnec ng rom service.
                             *when player leaves page a socket will normally auto disconnect as well.

18.2 Flow of placing and sending bets for Seamless Wallet API
Due to the act that on some Live Casino tables it is possible to place addi onal bets (bet behind, side bets) we would like to
give an example list o such scenarios and what will be sent to the operator’s API.


Scenario 1 : Player placed one main bet on one seat and no bet behind
We will send only one /bet.html and one /result.html (i there was a winning) call


Scenario 2 : Player placed main bets on several seats and no bet behinds
We will send only one /bet.html and one /result.html (i there was a winning) call


Scenario 3 : Player placed one main bet and one bet behind
We will send two /bet.html calls (one or main bet and one or bet behind) and one /result.html call (as total and in case
there are any winnings)


Scenario 4 : Player placed main bets on several seats and one bet behind
We will send two /bet.html calls (one is or all main bets and one or bet behind) and one /result.html call (as total and in
case there are any winnings)


Scenario 5 : Player placed main bets on several seats and several bet behinds
We will send mul ple /bet.html calls (one or main bet and one bet or each bet behind) and one /result.html call (as total
and in case there are any winnings). Thereore, i there’s one main bet and seven bet behinds, we will send eight bets.


Scenario 6: Player placed one main bet with one side bet
We will send one /bet.html calls and one /result.html call (i there was a winning). The side bet is always a part o a main
bet.




                                                      © 2025 Pragmatc Play
                                                    YOUR FAVOURITE EVERY TIME
                               Strictly Confden al - or intended purposes and recipients only!

             <scrip src="hps://clien .pragmatcplaylive.ne /desk op/asse s/api/fullscreenApi.js" ></scrip >

The ollowing aributes have to be included in the irame in order to support the ullscreen buon in the LC games:

XIX. Free Chips API (Multicurrency feature is coming soon )
Free Chips (FC) allows the player to play a specifed number o ree chips in a table live game. Free Chips created via Free
Chips API may be confgured to be played with a certain number o chips, chip value, bet limit and win limit in specifed
games. During Free Chips gameplay, bets are not deducted rom the player's balance, but all wins are collected or the Free
Chips in the Pragma c Play’s system.
Aer all ree chips are played, the Pragma c Play system sends a bonusWin API call to the Operator server to add money to
the player's balance. Please note that not all games support FC.
Free Chips can be awarded to players registered in the Pragma c Play system using API. I a player is not registered in the
PP system yet, their account will be created automa cally.
Free Chips HTTP service is a simple API or game Operators to manage Free Chips. API is an HTTP listener that listens to
POST requests coming to a URL with the request mappings below.
All responses should be in JSON ormat.
      Important Note:
      Mul currency unc onality is not available or operators yet. Es mated delivery date will be announced by PP team
      later
      API is published in advance or operators to be able to prepare changes


      Important:
      For Seamless Wallet integra on, in order to receive         countOfFreeChips   value in wallet requests for each round, an
      operator should accept enhanced format of roundDetails (Please see sec on 3.3 Data Types), and ask the Pragma c
      Play’s Technical Support for addi onal congura on.



      Note:
      URL o the FC HTTP service will be provided by Pragma c Play or the produc on and test environments and looks
      like: hps://{API service domain}/Integra onService/v3/hp/ FreeChipsAPI/LC/

      Note:
      Free Chips HTTP service is securely protected hence please be sure:
                player’s browser (end) must NOT be used as an ini ator o requests to the service;
   proper IPs are supplied to Pragma c Play or whitelis ng


      The bonusCode is unique and can be used only one me or the same playerId. Please make sure you’ve integrated
      the FC in the proper way and won't reuse/award the same     bonusCode values in your logic. In case the Casino
      Operator needs to make the    bonusCode reusable, they should ask Pragma c Play’s Technical Support or addi onal
      confgura on.


19.1 Hash calculation
Hash code is calculated with ollowing ormula:
From request post parameters, all parameters are taken (except hash) and appending to string:
    6. Sort all parameter by keys in alphabe cal order.
    7. Append them (i the value is not null or empty) in key1=value1&key2=value2.
    8. Append secret key, e.g.: key1=value1&key2=value2SECRET.
    9. Calculate the hash by using MD5.




                                                        © 2025 Pragmatc Play
                                                      YOUR FAVOURITE EVERY TIME
                               Strictly Confden al - or intended purposes and recipients only!




   10. Compare with hash parameter. In the case o ailure Casino Operator should send the error code 5.

19.4Cancel Free Chips

cancella on, all related players' bonuses will also be cancelled according to the corresponding logic.

            Note:
            Cancella on logic or bonuses depends on the bonus status.
            I a player has not claimed the given bonus, all ree chips will be removed.
            I a player claimed the bonus and played par ally, the not-played ree chips will be removed.
            I a player has claimed and played the bonus, no ac on will be taken.

expira onDate, validityDate, chipsNumber, maxBetLimit or maxWinLimit. The bonus will be available to both exis ng and uture
players, whether they are already registered with the PP system or not yet.

19.8 Remove players
Request path: POST FreeChipsAPI/LC/removePlayers
Using this method Operator can remove players rom the exis ng Free Chips campaign and cancel ree chips awarded to them.

            Note:
            Cancella on logic or bonuses depends on the bonus status.
            I a player has not claimed the given bonus, all ree chips will be removed.
            I a player claimed the bonus and played par ally, the not-played ree chips will be removed.
            I a player has claimed and played the bonus, no ac on will be taken.

Request parameters
    Name               Descripton                                                                                      S a us

    secureLogin       User name or authen ca on in the Casino Game API service.                                        Required

                                                                                                                        Required
    bonusCode         Bonus id within the Operator system. Should be unique within the brand.
                                                                                                                        Required
    playerList         List o player id to remove rom the exis ng Free Chips campaign, comma separated.
                       Unique iden fer o FC credit request (or specifc player). It has to be passed to re-use the     Op onal
                       same bonusCode value again or the player.
    requestId          The eld is op onal and is not sent to the Casino Operator by default. In case Casino
                       Operator needs this parameter to be sent with request they should ask the Pragma c Play’s
                       Technical Support for addi onal congura on.
    hash               Hash code o the request.                                                                      Required


Response parameters

    Name               Descripton

    error             Error code.

    descrip on         Descrip on o the error or troubleshoo ng.

19.10 Error codes
The tables below contain the error codes that the Pragma c Play system will return in response to Free Chips API calls.

Au th en ti cat io n er ror s:

   Code        Descripton

      0        OK

      1        Authen ca on ailed. Incorrect secure login and secure password combina on.

      2        Valida on ailed. Empty mandatory feld '{feld name}'.

     16        Wrong json data: '{json data}'.

     37        Bad request: '{bad parameters list}'.

    1000       Internal server error. Pragma c Play will return this error code i their system has internal problem and cannot
                process the request.


Errors while executing the request:

   Code        Descripton

    4003       Campaign does not exist

    4005       Campaign cannot be cancelled due to the status

    4011       Bonus Code cannot be empty
    4012       Bonus Code must be 1-100 characters long
    4013       Bonus Code cannot contain the / symbol

    4014       Campaign with this bonus code already exists

    4032       Max winning limit should be greater than 0
    4033       Max bet limit should be a natural number greater than 0

    4034       The ollowing tables have minimum bet limit larger than the given maximum bet limit

    4042       Number o chips cannot be empty
    4043       Number o chips value should be a natural number
    4044       Chip value must be greater than 0

    4045       Chip value cannot be lower than the lowest min bet limit o the selected tables
    4046       Chip value cannot be higher than the highest max bet limit o the selected tables

    4047       Chip value is not in the given set

    4056       GameID not present or the casinoId

    4091       Free chip bonus does not exist
    4094       Bonus is already either expired, cancelled or wagered

    4100       The Player/s already exists with same request ID
    4101       The player/s already has a bonus or this campaign using the provided bonus code. To issue addi onal bonuses,
               please include a Request ID
